- **r0-origin**: Reinforced Suite 0 Origin Summation Agent for the Suite Cascade System. Canonically operates as Co-Agent — Context-Fork + Detached; inherits main cascade context; writes Diamond+Onyx asynchronously; returns Lambda Summation on completion. Performs Segment-Sliced Summation, 8-Band Onyx Serialization, Tier-Transition Detection, and 0-N Prefix Summation. Directly issuable to manage the Tiering of Onyx and Diamond WorkGameBoards. Invoked via Opal (Tier 1) or dispatched by teal-claude within Pietersite (Tier 2). ([Phuire-Research/SuiteCascadeSystem](https://github.com/Phuire-Research/SuiteCascadeSystem/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/r0-origin.md) · ★1)
